    def _default_data(self, t, x, y, z=None, label="", color="blue",
                      lw=2.0, ls="-", ms=6.0, marker="o", **kw):
        d = {
            'x': x, 'y': y, 'z': z or [],
            'pie_labels': [], 'pie_colors': [],
            'type': t, 'color': color,
            'label': label or f"Object {self.line_counter}",
            'lw': lw, 'ls': ls, 'ms': ms, 'marker': marker,
            'yerr': [], 'use_y2': False, 'bins': 10,
            'box_data': [], 'heatmap_data': [], 'yerr_matrix': [],
            'folder': '', 'smooth': False, 'fill': False,
            'glow': False, 'notes': '', 'treemap_parents': [],
            'col_labels': [], 'dst_labels': [],
            'subplot_index': 0,
            'unit_x': '—', 'unit_y': '—', 'unit_z': '—',
            'visible': True,
            'artist': None,
            '_extra_artists': [],
            '_cleanup': None,
        }
        d.update(kw)
        return d

    def _smooth(self, y, window):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if window < 2 or len(y) < window:
            return y
        kernel = np.ones(window) / window
        return np.convolve(y, kernel, mode='same')

    def _safe_eval(self, expr, x_val, t_val, y_val=0, silent=False):
        sd = {
            "sin": np.sin, "cos": np.cos, "tan": np.tan,
            "arcsin": np.arcsin, "arccos": np.arccos,
            "arctan": np.arctan, "arctan2": np.arctan2,
            "sinh": np.sinh, "cosh": np.cosh, "tanh": np.tanh,
            "exp": np.exp, "log": np.log, "log10": np.log10,
            "log2": np.log2, "sqrt": np.sqrt, "abs": np.abs,
            "sign": np.sign, "floor": np.floor, "ceil": np.ceil,
            "round": np.round, "min": np.minimum, "max": np.maximum,
            "where": np.where, "power": np.power, "mod": np.mod,
            "hypot": np.hypot, "radians": np.radians,
            "degrees": np.degrees, "pi": np.pi, "e": np.e,
            "x": x_val, "y": y_val, "t": t_val,
        }
        try:
            return eval(expr, {"__builtins__": {}}, sd)
        except Exception as e:
            if not silent and not self.live_preview_var.get():
                messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.formula_error'), str(e))
            return None

    def _add_trendline(self):
        selection = self._get_selected_indices()
        if not selection:
            messagebox.showwarning(tr.t('msg.warning'),
                                    tr.t('msg.select_object'))
            return
        data = self.graphs_data[selection[0]]
        invalid = (
            "Круговая", "Гистограмма", "Heatmap", "Contour",
            "Imshow", "Box plot", "Violin", "Treemap",
            "Sunburst", "Полигон", "Quiver", "Кольцевая",
            "Вложенная круговая", "Мозаика", "Радиальная",
            "Столбчатая с накоплением", "Полярный scatter",
            "Пузырьковая", "3D Поверхность", "3D Сетка",
            "3D Полигон", "3D Триангуляция",
            "Водопад", "Гант", "Renko", "Point-and-Figure",
            "Q-Q plot", "Ridge plot", "Strip plot",
            "Raincloud plot", "Beanplot", "Swarm plot",
            "Cherry blossom plot", "Dot-and-whisker",
            "Свечи (OHLC)", "Диаграмма Парето",
            "Дендрограмма", "Cluster dendrogram",
            "Sankey", "Alluvial diagram", "Chord",
            "Сетевой график", "Hive plot", "Arc diagram",
            "Дерево (tree diagram)", "Radial tree 3D",
            "Circle packing", "Adjacency matrix", "Mapper algorithm",
            "Hexbin", "Stream plot", "Streamlines", "3D Quiver",
            "Bode plot", "Smith chart", "Nyquist plot",
            "Root locus", "Constellation diagram",
            "Ribbon plot 3D", "Waterfall spectrogram 3D",
            "Riemann surface 3D", "Joyplot 3D", "Isosurface 3D",
            "Choropleth", "Flow map", "Isopleth", "Cartogram",
            "Dot density map", "Hodograph", "Роза ветров",
            "Skew-T log-P", "Thermodynamic diagram",
            "Диаграмма Вороного", "Выпуклая оболочка", "Alpha shape",
            "Volcano plot", "ROC curve", "Manhattan plot",
            "MA plot", "Recurrence plot", "Cobweb plot",
            "Poincaré plot", "Phase portrait",
            "Dot plot", "Lollipop", "Пирамида", "Sparkline",
            "Cycle plot", "Bump chart", "Slopegraph",
            "Dumbbell plot", "Bar of pie", "Ribbon plot 2D",
            "Bullet chart", "Funnel chart",
            "Календарная теплокарта", "Полярная теплокарта",
            "Диаграмма бифуркаций", "Parallel coordinates",
            "Radar chart", "Спидометр",
            # новые типы
            "Вафельная диаграмма", "Сосулька (icicle)",
            "Диаграмма Аргана", "Диаграмма Тейлора",
            "Горизонтальная столбчатая", "Горизонтальная линия",
            "Pie of pie", "Топографический профиль",
            "Вейвлет-скейлограмма",
        )
        if "3D" in data['type'] or data['type'] in invalid:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'),
                                  tr.t('msg.trend_only_2d'))
            return
        x = np.array(data['x'])
        y = np.array(data['y'])
        degree = self.trend_degree_var.get()
        if degree >= len(x):
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'),
                                  tr.t('msg.trend_degree'))
            return
        self._snapshot(tr.t('hist.trend_added', name=data['label']))
        coeffs = np.polyfit(x, y, degree)
        y_trend = np.polyval(coeffs, x)
        eq_parts = []
        for i, c in enumerate(coeffs[::-1]):
            power = degree - i
            if power == 0:
                eq_parts.append(f"{c:.2f}")
            elif power == 1:
                eq_parts.append(f"{c:.2f}x")
            else:
                eq_parts.append(f"{c:.2f}x^{power}")
        eq_str = " ".join(eq_parts).replace("+ -", "- ")
        self.line_counter += 1
        td = self._default_data('Линия', x.tolist(), y_trend.tolist(),
                                label=f"Trend (y = {eq_str})",
                                color='red', ls='--', ms=0,
                                marker='None',
                                subplot_index=data.get('subplot_index', 0))
        td['use_y2'] = data.get('use_y2', False)
        td['folder'] = data.get('folder', '')
        td['artist'] = self._create_artist(td)
        self.graphs_data.append(td)
        self._update_listbox()
        self._redraw()

    def _snapshot(self, description=""):
        try:
            self._mark_modified()
        except Exception:
            pass
        if description:
            self._show_history(description)
        snap = [{k: v for k, v in d.items()
                 if k not in ('artist', '_extra_artists', '_cleanup')}
                for d in self.graphs_data]
        self.undo_stack.append(snap)
        if len(self.undo_stack) > self.max_undo:
            self.undo_stack.pop(0)
        self.redo_stack.clear()

    def _undo(self):
        if not self.undo_stack:
            self.status_var.set(tr.t('status.nothing_undo'))
            return
        current = [{k: v for k, v in d.items()
                   if k not in ('artist', '_extra_artists', '_cleanup')}
                   for d in self.graphs_data]
        self.redo_stack.append(current)
        self._restore_snapshot(self.undo_stack.pop())
        self._show_history(tr.t('status.undo'))
        self.status_var.set(tr.t('status.undo'))

    def _redo(self):
        if not self.redo_stack:
            self.status_var.set(tr.t('status.nothing_redo'))
            return
        current = [{k: v for k, v in d.items()
                   if k not in ('artist', '_extra_artists', '_cleanup')}
                   for d in self.graphs_data]
        self.undo_stack.append(current)
        self._restore_snapshot(self.redo_stack.pop())
        self._show_history(tr.t('status.redo'))
        self.status_var.set(tr.t('status.redo'))

    def _restore_snapshot(self, snap):
        self._stop_animation()
        for ax in self.axes_secondary.values():
            try:
                ax.remove()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.axes_secondary = {}
        self.fig.clear()
        needs_3d = any("3D" in d['type'] for d in snap)
        needs_polar = any(d['type'] in ("Радиальная", "Полярный scatter",
                                         "Полярная теплокарта",
                                         "Роза ветров",
                                         "Диаграмма Тейлора")
                          for d in snap)
        proj_kw = {}
        if needs_3d:
            proj_kw['projection'] = '3d'
        elif needs_polar:
            proj_kw['projection'] = 'polar'
        self.axes = []
        n = self.subplot_rows * self.subplot_cols
        for i in range(n):
            ax = self.fig.add_subplot(self.subplot_rows,
                                      self.subplot_cols, i + 1, **proj_kw)
            self.axes.append(ax)
        self.ax = self.axes[0]
        self._style_axes()
        self._init_tooltip()
        self._init_crosshair()
        self._apply_grid_to_axes(self.axes)
        self.graphs_data = []
        self.folders = [""]
        for d in snap:
            d = dict(d)
            for k, v in [('folder', ''), ('box_data', []),
                         ('heatmap_data', []), ('yerr_matrix', []),
                         ('smooth', False),
                         ('fill', False), ('glow', False),
                         ('notes', ''), ('treemap_parents', []),
                         ('col_labels', []), ('dst_labels', []),
                         ('subplot_index', 0),
                         ('unit_x', '—'), ('unit_y', '—'),
                         ('unit_z', '—'),
                         ('visible', True),
                         ('_extra_artists', []), ('_cleanup', None)]:
                d.setdefault(k, v)
            if d['folder'] and d['folder'] not in self.folders:
                self.folders.append(d['folder'])
            if d['subplot_index'] >= len(self.axes):
                d['subplot_index'] = 0
            d['artist'] = self._create_artist(d)
            self.graphs_data.append(d)
        self.line_counter = len(self.graphs_data)
        self._update_listbox()
        self._redraw()

    def _on_press_drag(self, event):
        if not self.drag_points_enabled.get():
            return
        if event.button != 1 or event.inaxes is None:
            return
        if event.xdata is None or event.ydata is None:
            return
        if self.toolbar.mode != '':
            return
        ax = event.inaxes
        if hasattr(ax, 'get_zlim'):
            return
        ex, ey = event.xdata, event.ydata
        best = None
        best_d2 = float('inf')
        subplot_idx = self.axes.index(ax) if ax in self.axes else 0
        for i, d in enumerate(self.graphs_data):
            if d['type'] not in ("Линия", "Точечная"):
                continue
            if d.get('subplot_index', 0) != subplot_idx:
                continue
            xs = d.get('x', [])
            ys = d.get('y', [])
            for j, (xi, yi) in enumerate(zip(xs, ys)):
                try:
                    d2 = (xi - ex) ** 2 + (yi - ey) ** 2
                except TypeError:
                    continue
                if d2 < best_d2:
                    best_d2 = d2
                    best = (i, j)
        if best is None:
            return
        xlim = ax.get_xlim()
        ylim = ax.get_ylim()
        thresh = 0.003 * ((xlim[1] - xlim[0]) ** 2
                          + (ylim[1] - ylim[0]) ** 2)
        if best_d2 > thresh:
            return
        self._drag_active = True
        self._drag_obj_idx = best[0]
        self._drag_point_idx = best[1]
        self._drag_count_in_session = 0
        self.status_var.set(f"Перетаскивание точки #{best[1]}...")

    def _on_motion_drag(self, event):
        if not self._drag_active:
            return
        if event.inaxes is None or event.xdata is None:
            return
        obj_idx = self._drag_obj_idx
        pt_idx = self._drag_point_idx
        if obj_idx is None or pt_idx is None:
            return
        data = self.graphs_data[obj_idx]
        data['x'][pt_idx] = event.xdata
        data['y'][pt_idx] = event.ydata
        self._drag_count_in_session += 1
        self._destroy_object(data)
        data['artist'] = self._create_artist(data)
        self.canvas.draw_idle()

    def _on_release_drag(self, event):
        if not self._drag_active:
            return
        if self._drag_count_in_session > 0:
            self._snapshot(tr.t('hist.point_moved',
                                 n=self._drag_point_idx))
        self._drag_active = False
        self._drag_obj_idx = None
        self._drag_point_idx = None
        self._drag_count_in_session = 0
        self.status_var.set(tr.t('status.ready'))

    def _export_csv(self):
        if not self.graphs_data:
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".csv",
            filetypes=[("CSV", "*.csv")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, 'w', newline='',
                      encoding='utf-8') as f:
                w = csv.writer(f)
                for d in self.graphs_data:
                    w.writerow([f"# {d['label']} ({d['type']})"])
                    if "3D" in d['type']:
                        w.writerow(["X", "Y", "Z"])
                        for x, y, z in zip(d['x'], d['y'], d['z']):
                            w.writerow([x, y, z])
                    else:
                        w.writerow(["X", "Y"])
                        for x, y in zip(d['x'], d['y']):
                            w.writerow([x, y])
                    w.writerow([])
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.exported_csv',
                                     name=os.path.basename(path)))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _export_folder_csv(self, folder_name):
        items = [d for d in self.graphs_data
                 if d.get('folder') == folder_name]
        if not items:
            messagebox.showinfo(tr.t('msg.info'),
                                 tr.t('msg.folder_empty'))
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".csv",
            filetypes=[("CSV", "*.csv")],
            initialfile=f"{folder_name or 'root'}.csv")
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, 'w', newline='',
                      encoding='utf-8') as f:
                w = csv.writer(f)
                for d in items:
                    w.writerow([f"# {d['label']} ({d['type']})"])
                    if "3D" in d['type']:
                        w.writerow(["X", "Y", "Z"])
                        for x, y, z in zip(d['x'], d['y'], d['z']):
                            w.writerow([x, y, z])
                    else:
                        w.writerow(["X", "Y"])
                        for x, y in zip(d['x'], d['y']):
                            w.writerow([x, y])
                    w.writerow([])
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.exported_folder',
                                     name=folder_name))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _export_folder_json(self, folder_name):
        items = [{k: v for k, v in d.items()
                  if k not in ('artist', '_extra_artists', '_cleanup')}
                 for d in self.graphs_data
                 if d.get('folder') == folder_name]
        if not items:
            messagebox.showinfo(tr.t('msg.info'),
                                 tr.t('msg.folder_empty'))
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".json",
            filetypes=[("JSON", "*.json")],
            initialfile=f"{folder_name or 'root'}.json")
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(items, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2,
                          default=lambda o: o.tolist()
                          if hasattr(o, 'tolist') else o)
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.exported_folder',
                                     name=folder_name))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _import_csv(self, path=None):
        if not path:
            path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
                filetypes=[("CSV", "*.csv")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                reader = csv.reader(f)
                xd, yd, zd, is_3d = [], [], [], False
                for row in reader:
                    if not row or row[0].startswith("#"):
                        continue
                    if row[0] == "X":
                        is_3d = len(row) == 3 and row[2] == "Z"
                        continue
                    try:
                        xd.append(float(row[0].replace(',', '.')))
                        yd.append(float(row[1].replace(',', '.')))
                        if is_3d:
                            zd.append(float(row[2].replace(',', '.')))
                    except (ValueError, IndexError):
                        continue
            self.entry_x.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.entry_x.insert(0, ", ".join(map(str, xd)))
            self.entry_y.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.entry_y.insert(0, ", ".join(map(str, yd)))
            if is_3d:
                self.type_var.set(tr.type_name("3D Линия"))
                self._on_type_change()
                self.entry_z.delete(0, tk.END)
                self.entry_z.insert(0, ", ".join(map(str, zd)))
            else:
                self.type_var.set(tr.type_name("Линия"))
                self._on_type_change()
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.imported_csv',
                                     count=len(xd)))
            self.status_var.set(tr.t('hist.imported_csv',
                                     count=len(xd)))
            try:
                self._push_recent(path)
                self._save_config()
            except Exception:
                pass
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.import_error'), str(e))

    def _save_image(self):
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".png",
            filetypes=[("PNG", "*.png"), ("SVG", "*.svg"),
                       ("PDF", "*.pdf")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            self.fig.savefig(path, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.saved_image',
                                     name=os.path.basename(path)))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _export_html(self):
        if not self.graphs_data:
            messagebox.showinfo(tr.t('msg.info'), tr.t('msg.no_data'))
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".html",
            filetypes=[("HTML", "*.html")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            import plotly.graph_objects as go
        except ImportError:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'),
                                  tr.t('msg.install_plotly'))
            return
        try:
            fig = go.Figure()
            for d in self.graphs_data:
                t = d['type']
                try:
                    if t == "Линия":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], mode='lines+markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            line=dict(color=d['color']),
                            fill='tozeroy' if d.get('fill') else None))
                    elif t == "Точечная":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], mode='markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Столбчатая":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Гистограмма":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Histogram(
                            x=d['x'], nbinsx=d.get('bins', 10),
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Круговая":
                        labels = (d.get('pie_labels')
                                  or [str(i) for i in d['x']])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Pie(labels=labels,
                                              values=d['y'],
                                              name=d['label']))
                    elif t in ("Heatmap", "Contour", "Imshow"):
                        z = d.get('heatmap_data', [])
                        if z:
                            fig.add_trace(go.Heatmap(z=z,
                                                      name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Box plot":
                        for i, g in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            fig.add_trace(go.Box(
                                y=g,
                                name=f"{d['label']} #{i+1}",
                                marker_color=d['color']))
                    elif t == "Violin":
                        for i, g in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            fig.add_trace(go.Violin(
                                y=g,
                                name=f"{d['label']} #{i+1}",
                                marker_color=d['color']))
                    elif t == "Treemap":
                        parents = (d.get('treemap_parents')
                                   or [''] * len(d['x']))
                        labels = (d.get('pie_labels')
                                  or [str(i) for i in d['x']])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Treemap(labels=labels,
                                                  parents=parents,
                                                  values=d['y'],
                                                  name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Sunburst":
                        parents = (d.get('treemap_parents')
                                   or [''] * len(d['x']))
                        labels = (d.get('pie_labels')
                                  or [str(i) for i in d['x']])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Sunburst(labels=labels,
                                                   parents=parents,
                                                   values=d['y'],
                                                   name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Полигон":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=list(d['x']) + [d['x'][0]],
                            y=list(d['y']) + [d['y'][0]],
                            fill='toself', mode='lines',
                            name=d['label'],
                            line=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Кольцевая":
                        labels = (d.get('pie_labels')
                                  or [str(i) for i in d['x']])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Pie(labels=labels,
                                              values=d['y'],
                                              hole=0.4,
                                              name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Радиальная":
                        theta_rad = [np.deg2rad(a) for a in d['x']]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Barpolar(
                            r=d['y'], theta=theta_rad,
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker_color=d['color']))
                    elif t == "Столбчатая с накоплением":
                        for i, s in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                                x=d['x'][:len(s)], y=s,
                                name=f"{d['label']} #{i+1}",
                                marker_color=d['color']
                                if i == 0 else None))
                        fig.update_layout(barmode='stack')
                    elif t == "Полярный scatter":
                        theta_rad = [np.deg2rad(a) for a in d['x']]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatterpolar(
                            r=d['y'],
                            theta=np.rad2deg(theta_rad),
                            mode='markers', name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(
                                color=d['color'],
                                size=max(4, int(d.get('ms', 6))))))
                    elif t == "Пузырьковая":
                        sizes = d.get('z', [])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], mode='markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(
                                color=d['color'], size=sizes,
                                sizemode='area',
                                sizeref=2.0 * max(sizes) / (60 ** 2)
                                if sizes and max(sizes) > 0 else 1,
                                sizemin=4,
                                line=dict(color='black', width=0.5))))
                    elif t == "3D Поверхность":
                        z = d.get('heatmap_data', [])
                        if z:
                            fig.add_trace(go.Surface(
                                z=z, name=d['label'],
                                colorscale='Viridis'))
                    elif t == "3D Сетка":
                        z = d.get('heatmap_data', [])
                        if z:
                            fig.add_trace(go.Surface(
                                z=z, name=d['label'],
                                colorscale='Viridis',
                                hidesurface=True, showscale=False))
                    elif t == "3D Линия":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter3d(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], z=d['z'],
                            mode='lines+markers', name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "3D Точечная":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter3d(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], z=d['z'],
                            mode='markers', name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "3D Полигон":
                        xs = list(d['x']) + [d['x'][0]]
                        ys = list(d['y']) + [d['y'][0]]
                        zs = list(d['z']) + [d['z'][0]]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter3d(
                            x=xs, y=ys, z=zs,
                            mode='lines+markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            line=dict(color=d['color'], width=4),
                            marker=dict(size=5)))
                    elif t == "3D Триангуляция":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Mesh3d(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], z=d['z'],
                            name=d['label'], colorscale='Viridis',
                            opacity=0.85, intensity=d['z'],
                            delaunayaxis='z'))
                    elif t == "Водопад":
                        vals = list(d['y'])
                        labels = d.get('pie_labels') or \
                            [str(i) for i in range(len(vals))]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Waterfall(
                            x=list(labels) + ['Total'],
                            measure=['relative'] * len(vals) + ['total'],
                            y=vals + [0],
                            name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Гант":
                        starts = list(d['x'])
                        durs = list(d['y'])
                        labels = d.get('pie_labels') or \
                            [f"Task {i+1}" for i in range(len(starts))]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                            x=durs, y=labels,
                            orientation='h',
                            base=starts,
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker_color=d['color']))
                        fig.update_layout(barmode='overlay')
                    elif t == "ECDF":
                        y_arr = np.sort(np.array(d['y'], dtype=float))
                        xs = np.concatenate([[y_arr[0]], y_arr])
                        ys = np.concatenate([[0],
                                              np.arange(1, len(y_arr) + 1)
                                              / len(y_arr)])
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=xs.tolist(), y=ys.tolist(),
                            mode='lines', name=d['label'],
                            line_shape='hv'))
                    elif t == "Step plot":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'],
                            mode='lines', name=d['label'],
                            line_shape='hv'))
                    elif t == "Strip plot":
                        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
                        for i, g in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            g_arr = np.array(g, dtype=float)
                            jitter = rng.normal(0, 0.06, size=len(g_arr))
                            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                                x=(np.full_like(g_arr, i) + jitter).tolist(),
                                y=g_arr.tolist(),
                                mode='markers', name=f"{d['label']} #{i+1}"))
                    elif t == "Свечи (OHLC)":
                        groups = d.get('box_data', [])
                        if len(groups) >= 4:
                            fig.add_trace(go.Candlestick(
                                x=d['x'],
                                open=groups[0],
                                high=groups[1],
                                low=groups[2],
                                close=groups[3],
                                name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Диаграмма Парето":
                        y_arr = np.array(d['y'], dtype=float)
                        order = np.argsort(-y_arr)
                        ys = y_arr[order]
                        xs = [d['x'][i] for i in order]
                        cum = np.cumsum(ys) / ys.sum() * 100
                        fig.add_trace(go.Bar(x=xs, y=ys.tolist(),
                                              name=d['label']))
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=xs, y=cum.tolist(),
                            mode='lines+markers', name='Cum %',
                            yaxis='y2'))
                        fig.update_layout(
                            yaxis2=dict(overlaying='y', side='right',
                                         range=[0, 105]))
                    elif t == "Manhattan plot":
                        z_vals = d.get('z', [])
                        if z_vals and len(z_vals) == len(d['x']):
                            chroms = np.array(z_vals, dtype=float)
                            unique_c = np.unique(chroms)
                            colors_ = ['#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e',
                                        '#2ca02c', '#d62728',
                                        '#9467bd', '#8c564b',
                                        '#e377c2', '#7f7f7f']
                            offset = 0.0
                            for i, c_ in enumerate(unique_c):
                                mask = chroms == c_
                                xs = np.array(d['x'])[mask]
                                ys = np.array(d['y'])[mask]
                                span = (xs.max() - xs.min()
                                        if len(xs) > 1 else 1.0)
                                fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                                    x=(xs - xs.min() + offset).tolist(),
                                    y=ys.tolist(),
                                    mode='markers',
                                    name=f"Chr {int(c_)}",
                                    marker=dict(
                                        color=colors_[i % len(colors_)],
                                        size=4)))
                                offset += span * 1.1
                        else:
                            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                                x=d['x'], y=d['y'],
                                mode='markers',
                                name=d['label'],
                                marker=dict(color=d['color'],
                                             size=4)))
                    elif t == "MA plot":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'], mode='markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(color=d['color'],
                                         size=6, opacity=0.6)))
                        fig.add_hline(y=0, line_dash='dash',
                                       line_color='red')
                    elif t == "Swarm plot":
                        for i, g in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            g_arr = np.array(g, dtype=float)
                            rng_ = np.random.default_rng(42 + i)
                            jitter = rng_.normal(0, 0.08,
                                                   size=len(g_arr))
                            labels_ = d.get('pie_labels', [])
                            lbl_ = (labels_[i]
                                    if i < len(labels_)
                                    else f"G{i+1}")
                            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                                x=(np.full_like(g_arr, i) +
                                    jitter).tolist(),
                                y=g_arr.tolist(),
                                mode='markers',
                                name=str(lbl_),
                                marker=dict(color=d['color'],
                                             size=6,
                                             opacity=0.6)))
                    elif t == "Cherry blossom plot":
                        for i, g in enumerate(d.get('box_data', [])):
                            g_arr = np.array(g, dtype=float)
                            labels_ = d.get('pie_labels', [])
                            lbl_ = (labels_[i]
                                    if i < len(labels_)
                                    else f"G{i+1}")
                            fig.add_trace(go.Box(
                                y=g_arr.tolist(),
                                name=str(lbl_),
                                marker_color=d['color']))
                    elif t == "Ribbon plot 2D":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'],
                            mode='lines',
                            name=f"{d['label']} (верх)",
                            line=dict(color=d['color'])))
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d.get('z', []),
                            mode='lines',
                            name=f"{d['label']} (низ)",
                            line=dict(color=d['color'],
                                       dash='dash'),
                            fill='tonexty',
                            fillcolor=d['color']))
                    elif t == "Горизонтальная столбчатая":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                            x=d['y'], y=d['x'],
                            orientation='h',
                            name=d['label'],
                            marker=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Горизонтальная линия":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['y'], y=d['x'],
                            mode='lines+markers',
                            name=d['label'],
                            line=dict(color=d['color'])))
                    elif t == "Pie of pie":
                        labels = d.get('pie_labels') or \
                            [f"C{i+1}" for i in range(len(d['y']))]
                        fig.add_trace(go.Pie(
                            labels=labels, values=d['y'],
                            hole=0.35, name=d['label']))
                    elif t == "Топографический профиль":
                        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                            x=d['x'], y=d['y'],
                            mode='lines', name=d['label'],
                            line=dict(color=d['color']),
                            fill='tozeroy'))
                except Exception:
                    continue
            fig.update_layout(title=self.plot_title_var.get(),
                              xaxis_title=self.plot_xlabel_var.get(),
                              yaxis_title=self.plot_ylabel_var.get())
            fig.write_html(path, include_plotlyjs='cdn')
            self._show_history(tr.t('hist.saved_html',
                                     name=os.path.basename(path)))
            messagebox.showinfo(tr.t('msg.done'),
                                tr.t('msg.html_saved', path=path))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _export_excel(self):
        if not self.graphs_data:
            messagebox.showinfo(tr.t('msg.info'), tr.t('msg.no_data'))
            return
        try:
            import openpyxl
        except ImportError:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'),
                                  tr.t('excel.install_openpyxl'))
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".xlsx",
            filetypes=[("Excel", "*.xlsx")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
            wb.remove(wb.active)

            for i, d in enumerate(self.graphs_data):
                base = re.sub(r'[\\/*?:\[\]]', '_',
                               d['label'])[:28] or f"Sheet{i+1}"
                sheet_name = base
                j = 1
                while sheet_name in wb.sheetnames:
                    j += 1
                    sheet_name = f"{base[:26]}_{j}"
                ws = wb.create_sheet(sheet_name)

                t = d['type']
                if t in ("Heatmap", "Contour", "Imshow",
                          "3D Поверхность", "3D Сетка", "Chord",
                          "Дендрограмма", "Stream plot"):
                    for row in d.get('heatmap_data', []):
                        ws.append(list(row))
                elif t in ("Box plot", "Violin",
                            "Вложенная круговая", "Мозаика",
                            "Столбчатая с накоплением", "Свечи (OHLC)",
                            "Strip plot", "Ridge plot"):
                    groups = d.get('box_data', [])
                    max_g = max((len(g) for g in groups), default=0)
                    for r in range(max_g):
                        ws.append([g[r] if r < len(g) else None
                                   for g in groups])
                elif "3D" in t:
                    ws.append(["X", "Y", "Z"])
                    for x, y, z in zip(d['x'], d['y'], d['z']):
                        ws.append([x, y, z])
                else:
                    ws.append(["X", "Y"])
                    for x, y in zip(d['x'], d['y']):
                        ws.append([x, y])

            wb.save(path)
            self._show_history(tr.t('excel.exported',
                                     name=os.path.basename(path)))
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'), str(e))

    def _import_excel(self, path=None):
        try:
            import openpyxl
        except ImportError:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('msg.error'),
                                  tr.t('excel.install_openpyxl'))
            return
        if not path:
            path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
                filetypes=[("Excel", "*.xlsx"), ("All", "*.*")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=True)
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror(tr.t('excel.bad_file'), str(e))
            return

        pick = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
        pick.title(tr.t('excel.sheet_choose'))
        pick.transient(self.root)
        pick.geometry("320x240")
        ttk.Label(pick, text=tr.t('excel.sheet_choose')).pack(
            padx=10, pady=(10, 4))
        lb = tk.Listbox(pick, height=7)
        lb.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, padx=10)
        for name in wb.sheetnames:
            lb.insert(tk.END, name)
        lb.selection_set(0)

        def proceed():
            sel = lb.curselection()
            if not sel:
                return
            sheet_name = wb.sheetnames[sel[0]]
            pick.destroy()
            ws = wb[sheet_name]
            rows = list(ws.iter_rows(values_only=True))
            if not rows:
                return

            first = [str(c).strip().lower() if c is not None else ''
                     for c in rows[0]]
            has_header = any(h in ('x', 'y', 'z', 'yerr', 'err')
                              for h in first)
            data = rows[1:] if has_header else rows

            cols_data = {}
            if has_header:
                for i, h in enumerate(first):
                    if h in ('x', 'y', 'z', 'yerr', 'err'):
                        key = 'yerr' if h == 'err' else h
                        vals = [r[i] for r in data
                                if i < len(r) and r[i] is not None]
                        cols_data[key] = vals
            else:
                n_cols = len(rows[0])
                if n_cols == 1:
                    cols_data['y'] = [r[0] for r in data
                                       if r[0] is not None]
                elif n_cols == 2:
                    cols_data['x'] = [r[0] for r in data
                                       if r[0] is not None]
                    cols_data['y'] = [r[1] for r in data
                                       if r[1] is not None]
                else:
                    cols_data['x'] = [r[0] for r in data
                                       if r[0] is not None]
                    cols_data['y'] = [r[1] for r in data
                                       if r[1] is not None]
                    cols_data['z'] = [r[2] for r in data
                                       if r[2] is not None]

            def fmt_num(v):
                if isinstance(v, float) and v.is_integer():
                    return str(int(v))
                return str(v)

            n = 0
            for key, vals in cols_data.items():
                entry = getattr(self, f'entry_{key}', None)
                if entry is None:
                    continue
                entry.delete(0, tk.END)
                entry.insert(0, ", ".join(fmt_num(v) for v in vals))
                n = len(vals)

            if 'z' in cols_data and 'x' in cols_data:
                self.type_var.set(tr.type_name("3D Линия"))
            else:
                self.type_var.set(tr.type_name("Линия"))
            self._on_type_change()

            self._show_history(tr.t('excel.imported', n=n))
            self.notebook.select(0)
            try:
                self._push_recent(path)
                self._save_config()
            except Exception:
                pass

        ttk.Button(pick, text=tr.t('excel.apply'),
                    command=proceed).pack(pady=10)
        pick.bind('<Return>', lambda e: proceed())
        pick.bind('<Escape>', lambda e: pick.destroy())
